    <script>\r\n
    (function() {\r\n
        'use strict';\r\n
\r\n
        // Prevent multiple initializations\r\n
        if (window.__ictserveThemeInit) return;\r\n
        window.__ictserveThemeInit = true;\r\n
\r\n
        const STORAGE_KEY = 'theme';\r\n
        const DEFAULT_THEME = 'light'; // Immutable default per v3.6.0\r\n
\r\n
        /**\r\n
         * Get theme from localStorage (simple string format)\r\n
         * @returns {string|null} Theme value or null\r\n
         */\r\n
        function getStoredTheme() {\r\n
            try {\r\n
                const value = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);\r\n
                return value === 'dark' ? 'dark' : (value === 'light' ? 'light' : null);\r\n
            } catch (e) {\r\n
                return null;\r\n
            }\r\n
        }\r\n
\r\n
        /**\r\n
         * Store theme in localStorage (simple string format)\r\n
         * @param {string} theme - Theme value ('light' or 'dark')\r\n
         */\r\n
        function setStoredTheme(theme) {\r\n
            const normalized = theme === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light';\r\n
            try {\r\n
                localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, normalized);\r\n
            } catch (e) {\r\n
                // Ignore storage errors (private browsing, quota exceeded)\r\n
            }\r\n
        }\r\n
\r\n
        /**\r\n
         * Apply theme to document root\r\n
         * @param {string} theme - Theme value ('light' or 'dark')\r\n
         */\r\n
        function applyTheme(theme) {\r\n
            const normalized = theme === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light';\r\n
            const root = document.documentElement;\r\n
\r\n
            if (normalized === 'dark') {\r\n
                root.classList.add('dark');\r\n
                root.setAttribute('data-theme', 'dark');\r\n
            } else {\r\n
                root.classList.remove('dark');\r\n
                root.setAttribute('data-theme', 'light');\r\n
            }\r\n
\r\n
            setStoredTheme(normalized);\r\n
        }\r\n
\r\n
        // Initialize theme: stored > default (light)\r\n
        // v3.6.0: No system preference detection\r\n
        const initialTheme = getStoredTheme() || DEFAULT_THEME;\r\n
        applyTheme(initialTheme);\r\n
\r\n
        // Expose global theme API for components\r\n
        window.ICTServeTheme = {\r\n
            get: function() {\r\n
                return getStoredTheme() || DEFAULT_THEME;\r\n
            },\r\n
            set: function(theme) {\r\n
                applyTheme(theme);\r\n
                // Dispatch event for reactive components\r\n
                window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('theme-changed', {\r\n
                    detail: {\r\n
                        theme: theme === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light'\r\n
                    }\r\n
                }));\r\n
            },\r\n
            toggle: function() {\r\n
                const current = this.get();\r\n
                const next = current === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';\r\n
                this.set(next);\r\n
                return next;\r\n
            }\r\n
        };\r\n
    })();\r\n

    <script>\n
        (function() {\n
            // Prevent multiple initializations\n
            if (window.__ictserveThemeToggleHandler) return;\n
            window.__ictserveThemeToggleHandler = true;\n
\n
            // Listen for Livewire theme-changed events\n
            document.addEventListener('livewire:init', function() {\n
                if (window.Livewire) {\n
                    Livewire.on('theme-changed', function(data) {\n
                        const theme = data?.theme || data?.[0]?.theme || 'light';\n
                        // Dispatch to app.js event listeners (no window.ICTServeTheme)\n
                        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('theme-changed', {\n
                            detail: {\n
                                theme: theme\n
                            }\n
                        }));\n
                    });\n
                }\n
            });\n
        })\n
        ();\n

<script>\n
    document.addEventListener('livewire:init', () => {\n
        // Auto-scroll to bottom when new messages arrive\n
        Livewire.on('announce', (event) => {\n
            const container = document.querySelector('[x-ref="messagesContainer"]');\n
            if (container) {\n
                setTimeout(() => {\n
                    container.scrollTop = container.scrollHeight;\n
                }, 100);\n
            }\n
        });\n
\n
        // Handle keyboard shortcuts\n
        document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {\n
            // ESC to close widget\n
            if (e.key === 'Escape') {\n
                const widget = document.querySelector('[x-data*="isOpen"]');\n
                if (widget && widget.__x.$data.isOpen) {\n
                    Livewire.dispatch('closeWidget');\n
                }\n
            }\n
        });\n
    });\n
\n
    // Alpine.js component definition\n
    Alpine.data('faqBotWidget', () => ({\n
        isOpen: false,\n
        isMinimized: false,\n
        announcement: '',\n
\n
        init() {\n
            // Initialize with default values - will be overridden by initializeWidget\n
        },\n
\n
        initializeWidget(initialIsOpen, initialIsMinimized, initialAnnouncement) {\n
            // Set initial values\n
            this.isOpen = initialIsOpen;\n
            this.isMinimized = initialIsMinimized;\n
            this.announcement = initialAnnouncement;\n
\n
            // Wait for Livewire to be ready before setting up watchers\n
            this.$nextTick(() => {\n
                // Check if $wire is available, if not wait for livewire:init\n
                if (typeof $wire !== 'undefined') {\n
                    this.setupLivewireSync();\n
                } else {\n
                    // Listen for Livewire initialization\n
                    document.addEventListener('livewire:init', () => {\n
                        this.setupLivewireSync();\n
                    });\n
                }\n
            });\n
\n
            // Handle announcements (this doesn't depend on $wire)\n
            this.$watch('announcement', value => {\n
                if (value) {\n
                    this.$dispatch('announce', { message: value });\n
                }\n
            });\n
\n
            // Handle focus management (this doesn't depend on $wire)\n
            this.$watch('isOpen', (open) => {\n
                if (open) {\n
                    this.focusFirst();\n
                } else {\n
                    this.$nextTick(() => this.$refs.toggleButton?.focus());\n
                }\n
            });\n
        },\n
\n
        setupLivewireSync() {\n
            // Sync with Livewire - only called when $wire is available\n
            if (typeof $wire !== 'undefined') {\n
                this.$watch(() => $wire.isOpen, (value) => this.isOpen = value);\n
                this.$watch(() => $wire.isMinimized, (value) => this.isMinimized = value);\n
                this.$watch(() => $wire.announcement, (value) => this.announcement = value);\n
            }\n
        },\n
\n
        focusableEls() {\n
            return [...(this.$refs.panel?.querySelectorAll('button, [href], input, select, textarea, [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])') || [])]\n
                .filter((el) => !el.disabled && el.offsetParent !== null);\n
        },\n
\n
        trapFocus(event) {\n
            if (!this.isOpen || this.isMinimized) return;\n
            const els = this.focusableEls();\n
            if (!els.length) return;\n
            const first = els[0];\n
            const last = els[els.length - 1];\n
            const active = document.activeElement;\n
            if (event.shiftKey && active === first) {\n
                event.preventDefault();\n
                last.focus();\n
            } else if (!event.shiftKey && active === last) {\n
                event.preventDefault();\n
                first.focus();\n
            }\n
        },\n
\n
        focusFirst() {\n
            this.$nextTick(() => {\n
